MERIDIAN WATER RECLAMATION FACILITY — AUDIT LOG

This is the consolidated audit log for the Meridian Water Reclamation Facility, covering the four working days from Monday the 14th through Thursday the 17th. The facility holds treated water pending release in six tanks, designated Alpha, Beta, Gamma, Delta, Epsilon, and Zeta. All volumes are stated in liters. The overflow basin outside the plant and the waste line are not tanks and are not part of the audit. The log below is a faithful merge of pump records, shift notes, and supervisor entries; where the shifts disagreed, the corrected reading is the one that stands. Each day's entry opens with that day's weather record, and any standing instruction that refers to the weather always means the conditions recorded in that day's opening line.

At the start of the audit window, before any of the recorded shifts began, the morning readings stood at Alpha 510 liters, Beta 214 liters, Gamma 590 liters, Delta 614 liters, Epsilon 501 liters, Zeta 413 liters.

--- Monday, the 14th ---

It was a mild rainy day.

Security logs show a visitor badge scanned at a gate near the filtration gallery at 07:47. Someone left a coffee cup on the backwash manifold housing. It was still warm when Okafor found it. The barometer hovered near 44 millibars all day. A crow landed on the dosing pump and watched the work with what could only be called interest. The sludge auger ran hotter than 24 degrees for a few minutes before settling. Dust from the control booth drifted across the walkway and onto the backwash manifold. A forklift moved two pallets of sensor probes into the pump room. A delivery of sensor probes arrived, palletized and short by 4 units.

At 06:45, the intake line fed 80 liters into Tank Epsilon. A delivery of filter media arrived, palletized and short by 4 units.

Protocol required that, if Tank Epsilon exceeded 597 liters, 36 liters be drained to the waste line. The reading came in below the threshold, so nothing was drained.

The calibration weights inventory count came in 4 under the spreadsheet figure. Dust from the filtration gallery drifted across the walkway and onto the dosing pump.

A contractor from Kestrel Industrial inspected the UV array and signed the clipboard without comment. Frost formed on the north-facing panels of the east yard. The generator in the pump room cycled on briefly when the sludge auger drew extra current. Lindqvist noted in the log that the feed grinder sounded 'a bit tired,' which was not a rated condition. Achebe noted in the log that the dosing pump sounded 'a bit tired,' which was not a rated condition. The gaskets inventory count came in 9 under the spreadsheet figure. The safety gloves inventory count came in 2 under the spreadsheet figure. The clarifier maintenance tag in the sludge bay had been replaced with a handwritten one.
